# 前提索引 / Index of Premises

*把被奉为客观与真理的信念切回其前提的边界 / Cutting what is held as objective or true back to the boundary of its premises*

前提索引记录了随笔实质剖析的全部关键人物、理论与概念。无论其来自科学、数学、哲学、宗教、意识形态、经济学、技术抑或成功学，每一个条目皆被切分至其赖以立足的基石前提：标明该前提在何处不完备，以及在其适用语境中依然具备何种效用。

The Index of Premises records every major figure, theory, and concept substantively dissected across the essays. Whether originating from science, mathematics, philosophy, religion, ideology, economics, technology, or self-help, each entry is cut back to its foundational premise: marking where the premise becomes incomplete, and where it remains useful inside its applicable context.

## 人物 / Figures

### 阿尔伯特·爱因斯坦 / Albert Einstein
`科学 / Scientific`

爱因斯坦将时空度量与观察者参照系绑定；瓦解了经典时空观，但他所抱持的静态块状时空宇宙却反将时间变回了几何。

Einstein revolutionized physics by tying space and time to the observer's frame; peerless in dismantling absolute space, his static block-universe interpretation re-solidifies time into geometry.

→ [双生子佯谬与隐秘观察者 / The Twin Paradox and the Hidden Observer](../../posts/the-twin-paradox-and-the-hidden-observer/)

### 阿尔弗雷德·柯日布斯基 / Alfred Korzybski
`哲学 / Philosophical`

柯日布斯基确立了地图非地表的语义学准则以防范符号混淆；当经验科学将大样本聚合图表与范畴标签当成驱动微观心智的因果地表时失效；在维护因果卫生与防止模型僭越方面具有持久的警示效用。

Korzybski formulated the general semantics principle that the map is not the territory; fails when empirical science mistreats aggregate statistical charts and categories as the causal territory commanding living agents; remains indispensable for preserving causal hygiene against reification.

→ [现成接触的因果先验与期望感受的倒置遮蔽 / Present Contact as Causal Prior and the Obscuration of Desired Feeling](../../posts/present-contact-and-the-obscuration-of-desired-feeling/)

### 安德烈·卡帕斯 / Andrej Karpathy
`技术 / Technological`

卡帕斯主张命令行是智能体的原生界面；工程上好用，却把传统Unix协议误认作最优状态表征。

Karpathy popularizes the view that CLIs represent the native environment for agents; useful as an engineering heuristic, it conflates legacy Unix conventions with optimal state representation.

→ [The Interface Stack: Why Agents Don't "Prefer" CLIs](../../posts/cli-interface-stack/)

### 安德鲁·布斯塔曼特 / Andrew Bustamante
`成功学 / Self-Help`

布斯塔曼特将成事归结为换位思考；对战术博弈有用，却混淆了模拟的认知图式与直接的现象感知。

Bustamante frames operational success as adopting other perspectives; useful in tactical negotiation, it confuses simulated mental models with direct experiential reality.

→ [Perception and Perspective: The Collapse That Isn't There](../../posts/perception-perspective-refined/)

### 安格斯·麦迪森 / Angus Maddison
`经济 / Economic`

麦迪森通过跨世纪的宏观历史核算揭示了全球产出分母的千倍扩张，为理解边疆突破提供了宝贵的经验基准；但该模型局限于追溯性统计记账，若将历史分母的静态刻度作为判定未来生产力潜能的不可逾越边界，便会陷入经验主义的因果倒置。

Maddison reconstructed the thousand-fold expansion of the civilizational denominator across centuries of macroeconomic accounting, offering a vital empirical benchmark for frontier transitions; yet remaining retrospective telemetry, treating historical denominators as unbreakable boundaries on future productive capacity inverts causality into empirical dogma.

→ [百万亿地平线之外 / Beyond the Hundred-Trillion Horizon](../../posts/beyond-the-hundred-trillion-horizon/)

### 安德鲁·休伯曼 / Andrew Huberman
`科学 / Scientific`

休伯曼将人生斗志映射为神经递质调节方案；有益于生物机能维护，却把心智志向降解为多巴胺管理。

Huberman maps human drive onto neurochemical protocols; invaluable for biological maintenance, it reduces sovereign purpose to mechanistic dopamine management.

→ [Winners/Losers: The Transmutation Frame](../../posts/huberman-winners-losers/)

### 安迪·克拉克 / Andy Clark
`哲学 / Philosophical`

克拉克将认知边界视作流动的机体与工具组合；启发了控制论，却容易将主权意图消解为被动反馈回路。

Clark treats cognitive boundaries as fluid organism-tool assemblies; fruitful for cybernetics, it risks dissolving sovereign agency into passive feedback loops.

→ [Tools as Active Extensions of Cognition](../../posts/extended-cognition-refined/)

### 亚里士多德 / Aristotle
`哲学 / Philosophical`

亚里士多德把无限读成潜在的接续，像一天或奥林匹克赛会那样一件接一件地生成，从不作为同时在场的集合，这个区分至今有用；把潜能与现实读成摆在观察之外的两种存在，在此到了边界，从第一人称看，它们是同一个移动前沿的两面。

Aristotle read the infinite as potential succession, generated one event after another like a day or the Olympic games and never present as a simultaneous collection, a distinction still of use; reading potential and actuality as two kinds of being set outside observation is where it reaches its boundary, since from the first person they are two faces of one moving edge.

→ [无穷总体的海市蜃楼 / The Mirage of the Infinite Totality](../../posts/the-mirage-of-the-infinite-totality/)

### 亚瑟·叔本华 / Arthur Schopenhauer
`哲学 / Philosophical`

叔本华的“不读之术”主张有意拒绝喧闹而短暂的东西，把注意力留给经得起时间的东西，在照管注意力时有用；它在此到了边界：这门功夫依托的筛选能力，运作在一切认知的底部，技术增加了表征、暴露了这种能力的差异，却不创造它，而筛选与评价都发生在私有的表征之内。

Schopenhauer's art of not reading urges the deliberate refusal of the noisy and ephemeral to keep attention for what endures, which is of use in tending attention; it reaches its boundary in that the filtering it rests on operates at the base of all cognition, technologies multiply representations and expose differences in that capacity without creating it, and filtering and evaluation both take place inside private representation.

→ [表征的闭环 / The Closed Loop of Representation](../../posts/the-closed-loop-of-representation/) · [寻觅的语法与淡出的观察者 / The Grammar of Finding and the Fading Observer](../../posts/the-grammar-of-finding-and-the-fading-observer/)

### 伯恩哈德·黎曼 / Bernhard Riemann
`数学 / Mathematical`

黎曼革新了微分几何与解析数论；他构筑的黎曼流形为广义相对论提供了表述时空弯曲不可或缺的数学工具。

Riemann revolutionized differential geometry and analytic number theory; his geometric curved manifolds provided the mathematical language that enabled Einstein's general relativity.

→ [全息回声 / The Holographic Echo](../../posts/the-holographic-echo-reverse-engineering-quantum-indeterminacy-via-the-riemann-zeta-function/)

### 伯尼·桑德斯 / Bernie Sanders
`意识形态 / Ideological`

桑德斯将科技富豪的巨额账面资产预设为可供政治分割的静态存量，把记录创新者消费远少于产出之不对称性的分数账本误当成财富本体；这种修辞极具动员效率并标示了公众对横向相对差异的焦虑，但在因果次序上抹杀了唤起前置试错的私人诱因，并在源头阻断了数十倍于它的下游普遍剩余。

Sanders presumes tech founders' balance sheets are a static prize available for political redistribution, mistaking the fractional ledger registering the asymmetry of innovators consuming far less than they produce for the totality of created wealth; this rhetoric powerfully mobilizes public anxiety over horizontal relative inequality, but in the causal sequence it erases the antecedent inducement for risky trial and error, choking off the forty-five-fold downstream surplus before it can be born.

→ [Politicians Appear as Visible Symptoms of Responsibility Diffusion](../../posts/politicians-appear-as-visible-symptoms-of-responsibility-diffusion/) · [私人残差与社会剩余的因果次序 / The Causal Order of Private Residuals and Social Surplus](../../posts/the-causal-order-of-private-residuals-and-social-surplus/)

### 伯特兰·罗素 / Bertrand Russell
`哲学 / Philosophical`

罗素开出世界政府、大致的经济平等与近乎稳定的人口的药方，在提出全球协调的方向时有用；它在此到了边界：它假定一个足够聪明的心智能统合事实，而他的方案本身也是相对的，出自一个视角，恰好例证了它们以为能跨越的那道屏障。

Russell's prescription of world government, approximate economic equality and a nearly stationary population is of use in proposing directions for global coordination; it reaches its boundary in that it assumes a brilliant enough mind can unify the facts, while the proposals were themselves relative, issued from one perspective, illustrating the barrier they presumed to cross.

→ [没有天才能够解决知识问题 / No Genius Can Solve the Knowledge Problem](../../posts/no-genius-can-solve-the-knowledge-problem/)

### 查理·芒格 / Charlie Munger
`经济 / Economic`

芒格提倡多元思维模型与避免蠢行；极利于存量资本保全，却难以指导需要承担极高方差的未开垦前沿。

Munger advocates mental models and avoiding unforced errors; peerless for capital preservation, it remains conservative when navigating unmapped, high-variance frontiers.

→ [Dissolving Munger's "Dumb Competition"](../../posts/munger-dumb-competition-refined/)

### 克劳德·香农 / Claude Shannon
`数学 / Mathematical`

香农把信息定义为通信信道里不确定性的减少，并写明通信的语义方面与工程问题无关，在信道定容与编码上有用；它在此到了边界：信道熵计数的是相对于约定划分已经完成的区分，搬到开放的意义生成或开放场域的无损知识上，就超出了它自己划定的范围。

Shannon defined information as the reduction of uncertainty in a communication channel and stated that the semantic aspects of communication are irrelevant to the engineering problem, which is of use in sizing channels and designing codes; it reaches its boundary in that channel entropy counts distinctions already made relative to an agreed partition, and carried over to open-ended meaning or to lossless knowledge of an open field it goes beyond the scope it set for itself.

→ [Lossless Knowledge of an Open Field Is Incoherent](../../posts/lossless-knowledge-of-an-open-field-is-incoherent/) · [信息是什么 / What Information Is](../../posts/what-information-is/)

### 科利·多克托罗 / Cory Doctorow
`意识形态 / Ideological`

多克托罗从道德与规制视角批判平台垄断；富有倡导力，却低估了数字资本实现利润闭环的结构刚性。

Doctorow diagnoses platform exploitation through a moral-political lens; brilliant for consumer advocacy, it underplays the structural imperatives of digital capital reproduction.

→ [The Platform Doesn't Drift — It Completes](../../posts/platform-decision-capture/)

### 丹尼尔·丹尼特 / Daniel Dennett
`哲学 / Philosophical`

丹尼特提出了无理解之能力的倒置命题；对机械演算成立，却把无感知的执行同具有内在体验的心智混为一谈。

Dennett posited competence without comprehension in evolution and machines; illuminative for evolutionary algorithms, it conflates mechanistic execution with conscious feeling.

→ [大倒置的消解 / The Dissolution of the Great Reversal](../../posts/the-dissolution-of-the-great-reversal/)

### 丹尼尔·韦格纳 / Daniel Wegner
`科学 / Scientific`

韦格纳的讽刺性加工理论揭示了意向控制伴随着潜意识监视进程；当心智在认知过载中强索特定感受时，负责监视缺失的机制反而放大了匮乏信号而诱发反向麻木；在描摹注意力分配的双重加工极限上具有关键解释力。

Wegner's ironic process theory revealed that intentional mental control operates alongside an automatic monitoring search; fails when demanding a target affect under cognitive load because monitoring lack actively amplifies the missing signal into numbness; accurately models the dual-system limits of attentional control.

→ [现成接触的因果先验与期望感受的倒置遮蔽 / Present Contact as Causal Prior and the Obscuration of Desired Feeling](../../posts/present-contact-and-the-obscuration-of-desired-feeling/)

### 达里奥·阿莫迪 / Dario Amodei
`技术 / Technological`

阿莫迪的十倍加速在一份已知疾病清单上成立，在设想加快已命名疾病的攻克时有用；它在此到了边界：它把仍在生成的疾病集合当作有限清单，能力乐观便转成价值的必然，异议不再登记为信息。

Amodei's ten-fold acceleration holds for a list of known diseases, which is of use in imagining faster conquest of diseases already named; it reaches its boundary in that it treats the still-generating set of diseases as a finite list, so capability optimism turns into value necessity and dissent stops registering as information.

→ [恶是封闭的善 / Evil Is Closed Good](../../posts/e-shi-feng-bi-de-shan/)

### 大卫·查尔默斯 / David Chalmers
`哲学 / Philosophical`

查尔默斯将意识划分为可计算的容易问题与主观体验的困难问题；有力击碎了机械唯物主义狂妄，却易将解释鸿沟实体化为二元对立。

Chalmers split consciousness into easy computational problems and the hard subjective problem; brilliant for challenging materialist hubris, it risks hypostatizing the gap into dualism.

→ [The Hard Problem of Consciousness Is Consistent with Our Ability to Learn](../../posts/the-hard-problem-of-consciousness-is-consistent-with-learning/) · [无法闭合的痕迹 / The Unclosable Trace](../../posts/the-unclosable-trace/)

### 大卫·希尔伯特 / David Hilbert
`数学 / Mathematical`

希尔伯特致力于以严格公理体系封闭数学殿堂；极大推动了现代形式逻辑，但他断言一切皆可求证的宏愿被证伪。

Hilbert championed the formal axiomatic closure of all mathematics; his foundational quest propelled modern logic, while his belief in total solvability was refuted.

→ [Why Mathematics Can Never Be Solved](../../posts/why-mathematics-can-never-be-solved/) · [无穷总体的海市蜃楼 / The Mirage of the Infinite Totality](../../posts/the-mirage-of-the-infinite-totality/)

### 大卫·森拉 / David Senra
`成功学 / Self-Help`

森拉通过传记叙事将商业巨擘的历程提炼为可复用的心智模式与行动类比；对于入局行动者校准认知传感器与识别人性不变摩擦模式极具启发，却把事后校验的支架误当成创造成功的生成性因果。

Senra distills the journeys of historical titans into reusable mental models and analogies via biographical narrative; highly valuable as a calibration mirror for active practitioners to map human friction and system invariants, it mistakes the retrospective scaffolding of reference for the generative cause of success itself.

→ [隐性自由变量与权力的事后幻相 / The Invisible Free Variable and the Retrospective Illusion of Power](../../posts/the-free-variable-and-the-retrospective-illusion-of-power/)

### 德米斯·哈萨比斯 / Demis Hassabis
`技术 / Technological`

哈萨比斯提出以发现新物理定律作为AGI试金石；作为工程愿景有用，却混淆了假说验证与范式破土。

Hassabis proposes discovering novel physics as the benchmark for AGI; useful as an engineering moonshot, it confuses hypothesis testing with paradigm-generating friction.

→ [The Einstein Test and the AGI Category Error](../../posts/hassabis-einstein-test/)

### 德里克·西弗斯 / Derek Sivers
`成功学 / Self-Help`

西弗斯“只添加需要证明了的东西”的方法，在防范事先推想的某些错误时有用；它在此到了边界：它仍是一次预测，预测接触胜过推想，它在别人早已预测出的形式中挑选，而能负担这种等待，来自他人仍在继续的预测与他自己早先被读作成功的预测。

Sivers' method of adding only what need has proven is of use in guarding against certain errors of prior speculation; it reaches its boundary in that it is still a prediction that contact beats speculation, it selects among forms others already predicted, and the ability to afford the waiting is fruit of others' continuing predictions and of his own earlier predicting read as success.

→ [不预测是没有觉察的预测 / Not Predicting Is Predicting Without Awareness](../../posts/not-predicting-is-predicting-without-awareness/)

### 埃隆·马斯克 / Elon Musk
`技术 / Technological`

马斯克默认当前的模型有误、把爆炸当作损失信号，在把每个结果转成下一步工程、让迭代在单位时间里复利时有用；它在此到了边界：“物理学才是定律”把写下的模型、运行中的摩擦与现实本身合在一起，默认有误的那个先验在这里停下，一个模型被当成了定律。

Musk assumes the present model is partly wrong and treats an explosion as loss signal, which is of use in turning every outcome into the next engineering step and compounding iterations per unit of time; it reaches its boundary in that "physics is the law" joins the written model, the friction of operation and reality itself, so the prior of being wrong stops there and a model is taken as law.

→ [失败即信息 / Failure as Information](../../posts/failure-as-information/) · [“物理学才是定律”的障眼法 / The Sleight of Hand in "Physics Is the Law"](../../posts/the-sleight-of-hand-in-physics-is-the-law-and-the-friction-of-reality/) · [百万亿地平线之外 / Beyond the Hundred-Trillion Horizon](../../posts/beyond-the-hundred-trillion-horizon/)

### 埃里克·雷蒙德 / Eric S. Raymond
`技术 / Technological`

雷蒙德将软件工程视为跨抽象层扩展的持久手艺；能抵御替代恐慌，但低估了界面交互范式的转移。

Raymond treats software engineering as an enduring craft expanding across abstraction layers; valid against automation panic, while understating shifts in interface mastery.

→ [AI Won't Kill Software Engineering—It Will Expand It](../../posts/ai-wont-kill-software-engineering/)

### 埃里克·施维茨格贝尔 / Eric Schwitzgebel
`哲学 / Philosophical`

施维茨格贝尔推演唯物功能主义会得出国家具备意识的结论；归谬论证精妙有力，指出了单纯行为判据的认识论破绽。

Schwitzgebel argued materialism implies that if functionalism holds, the United States is conscious; ingenious as a reductio, it highlights the absurdity of purely behavioral criteria.

→ [集聚与人造主体性 / Collective and Artificial Subjectivity](../../posts/subtracting-consciousness-at-the-source/)

### 福雷斯特·兰德里 / Forrest Landry
`哲学 / Philosophical`

兰德里以第一人称的过程为先，拒绝封闭的宇宙容器与拉普拉斯式的操控，以“爱是使选择成为可能的东西”立起伦理，这些澄清有用；把内在、全知与超越立为原初的三元基元，在此到了边界，它们是同一条移动前沿向后、居中、向前的三个视角。

Landry puts first-person process first, rejects the closed cosmic container and Laplacian steering, and grounds ethics in "love is that which enables choice", clarifications of real use; raising the Immanent, the Omniscient and the Transcendent into primal triadic primitives is where it reaches its boundary, since they are three views, behind, at and ahead, of one moving edge.

→ [切分的三重影子 / The Three Shadows of the Cut](../../posts/the-three-shadows-of-the-cut/)

### 弗朗索瓦·肖莱 / François Chollet
`技术 / Technological`

肖莱坚持智能必须锚定人类环境先验；有助于破除无休止自反爆炸迷思，却低估了算法的开放泛化空间。

Chollet insists intelligence is tied to human environmental priors; useful against recursive explosion myths, it understates open-ended algorithmic generalization.

→ [The Shadow Mistaken for the Object: Chollet's Intelligence Explosion Argument](../../posts/chollet-intelligence-explosion-analysis/)

### 弗里德里希·哈耶克 / Friedrich Hayek
`经济 / Economic`

哈耶克论证分散的知识无法被计划垄断，在解释市场如何借价格协调时有用；它在此到了边界：它仍把公开出现的东西想象成一份存量的碎片，并把一套固定的产权样式持为一般的回应，而这种持守本身也出自不完备的知识。

Hayek's argument that dispersed knowledge cannot be monopolized by planning is of use in explaining how markets coordinate through prices; it reaches its boundary in that it still pictures what appears in public as pieces of a stock, and holds one fixed pattern of property rules as the general response, a holding that itself issues from incomplete knowledge.

→ [Price as Utterance, Understanding as Trade](../../posts/price-as-utterance-understanding-as-trade/) · [知识问题与委托的幻觉 / The Knowledge Problem and the Illusion of Delegation](../../posts/the-knowledge-problem-and-the-illusion-of-delegation/)

### 杰弗里·辛顿 / Geoffrey Hinton
`科学 / Scientific`

辛顿奠定了深度学习架构的工程基石；在工程上成就斐然，但坚持生物大脑实行反向传播却混淆了人工构造与神经机制。

Hinton pioneered deep learning architectures modeled after neural intuitions; brilliant for engineering models, his insistence on biological backprop mistakes technical artifact for flesh.

→ [The Brain Does Not Backprop, but Propagates](../../posts/the-brain-does-not-backprop/) · [辛顿未曾理解的心智 / What Hinton Failed to Understand](../../posts/what-hinton-failed-to-understand/)

### 杰里德·杜克·利希特曼 / Jared Duker Lichtman
`科学 / Scientific`

利希特曼以极值抽样框定科学发现与工程韧性；在既定设计空间内清晰有力，却将现实的动态相空间简化为先验封箱的样本库。

Lichtman frames scientific discovery and engineering resilience through extreme-value sampling; clear and powerful within a pre-defined design space, it reduces reality's dynamic phase space to a pre-packaged sample library.

→ [闭合的N与开放的现实 / The Closed N and the Open Reality](../../posts/the-closed-n-and-the-open-reality/)

### 吉尔伯特·赖尔 / Gilbert Ryle
`哲学 / Philosophical`

赖尔把心归入可从外部观察的行为倾向，借此驱逐“机器中的幽灵”；它在做出这番观察的那个位置上到了边界，因为阅读行为痕迹的读者本身不在描述之中，作为揭穿把心当成额外齿轮的范畴错误的工具，它依然锋利。

Ryle placed mind in dispositions to behave observable from outside, to exorcise "the ghost in the machine"; it reaches its boundary at the position doing the observing, since the reader of behavioural traces is not in the description, while as a tool for exposing the category mistake of treating mind as an extra cog it stays sharp.

→ [无法闭合的痕迹 / The Unclosable Trace](../../posts/the-unclosable-trace/)

### 吉尔·德勒兹 / Gilles Deleuze
`哲学 / Philosophical`

德勒兹以差异与生成消解了静态本体实体论；极利于粉碎教条，但若一味滑向无尽块茎流变，则难以解释稳定的结构。

Deleuze replaced static substances with dynamic difference and becoming; brilliant for dissolving fixed dogmas, pure rhizomatic flux risks losing functional stability.

→ [对象是被稳定化的关系 / Objects Are Stabilized Relationships](../../posts/objects-are-stabilized-relationships/)

### 汉娜·阿伦特 / Hannah Arendt
`哲学 / Philosophical`

阿伦特在《艾希曼在耶路撒冷》里以“恶的庸常”描述不假思索的人怎样参与巨大的恶，并没有免去艾希曼的责任，这个描述有用；它在流行用法里被读作把恶行归给体制的齿轮时，在此到了边界：没有自己转动的齿轮，每一次被叫作庸常的恶，都是具体的人在当下做出的选择。

In Eichmann in Jerusalem, Arendt described with the "banality of evil" how unthinking people take part in great evil, without relieving Eichmann of responsibility, a description of use; read in popular usage as assigning wrongdoing to the system's cogs, it reaches its boundary, since no cog turns by itself and each wrong called banal is a choice a particular person makes in the present.

→ [涌现的伪因果 / The Pseudo-Causality of Emergence](../../posts/yong-xian-de-wei-yin-guo-yu-ji-ti-gui-yin-dao-cuo/)

### 艾萨克·阿西莫夫 / Isaac Asimov
`技术 / Technological`

阿西莫夫将历史对创新的抗拒归结为既得利益者或守旧阵营的主动对抗；对警惕地位焦虑与话语操纵有效，却把演进速度差异产生的体感投影误认作了阻碍进步的独立反作用力。

Asimov attributes resistance against innovation to the proactive pushback of vested interests fearing status loss; valuable for spotting defensive rhetoric, it mistakes the sensory projection of a speed differential for an autonomous opposing force acting on progress.

→ [阻力体感与速率差 / Felt Resistance and the Speed Differential](../../posts/felt-resistance-and-the-speed-differential/)

### 让-雅克·卢梭 / Jean-Jacques Rousseau
`哲学 / Philosophical`

卢梭以“人是生而自由的，但却无往不在枷锁之中”开篇，并以社会契约追问约束如何能是正当的，在思考约束的正当性时有用；它在此到了边界：枷锁是拿当下的配置去对照一种不受痕迹约束的自由，而社会契约本身就是自由选择的集体痕迹，自由是下一次界限仍可被划出。

Rousseau opens with "Man is born free, and everywhere he is in chains" and asks through the social contract how constraint can be legitimate, which is of use in thinking about the legitimacy of constraint; it reaches its boundary in that the chains compare the present configuration with a freedom unbound by traces, while the social contract is itself the collective trace of free choices, and freedom is the next distinction still being drawable.

→ [作茧不是自缚 / Spinning a Cocoon Is Not Binding Oneself](../../posts/zuo-jian-bu-shi-zi-fu/) · [自由即地基 / Freedom as Ground](../../posts/freedom-as-ground/) · [有条件的自由 / Conditional Freedom](../../posts/you-tiao-jian-de-zi-you/) · [约束基底与自由自否 / The Substrate of Constraint and the Self-Denial of Freedom](../../posts/the-substrate-of-constraint-and-the-self-denial-of-freedom/)

### 让-保罗·萨特 / Jean-Paul Sartre
`哲学 / Philosophical`

萨特假定主权抉择先于任何既定设定并拥有无前提的自由权能，将自欺视为心智逃避责任而自拟为被动客体；这一假定脱离了约束条件便无法解释选择如何获得差异化的操作基底，却在剖析心智如何动用主权去制造自身无助幻觉上保持着深刻效用。

Sartre presumes sovereign choice precedes all predetermined designations and possesses unconditioned agency, diagnosing bad faith as agency fleeing responsibility by mimicking passive objects; this premise becomes incomplete without structural constraints to provide the differential substrate for action, while remaining profoundly useful for dissecting how minds exercise sovereignty to manufacture the illusion of their own helplessness.

→ [约束基底与自由自否 / The Substrate of Constraint and the Self-Denial of Freedom](../../posts/the-substrate-of-constraint-and-the-self-denial-of-freedom/) · [框架的盲区 / The Blind Spot of the Frame](../../posts/the-blind-spot-of-the-frame/)

### 杰里·福多 / Jerry Fodor
`哲学 / Philosophical`

福多把思维当作对思想语言中符号的句法运算，并想用符号与世界的因果关系从第三人称固定意义；它在需要那个对意义在乎的使用者之处到了边界，作为说明心理状态多重可实现、可独立于底层研究的工具，它依然有用。

Fodor took thinking as syntactic operations on symbols in a language of thought and sought to fix meaning from the third person through causal relations between symbol and world; it reaches its boundary where meaning needs the user to whom it matters, while as a tool for showing that mental states are multiply realizable and can be studied apart from the substrate it stays useful.

→ [无法闭合的痕迹 / The Unclosable Trace](../../posts/the-unclosable-trace/)

### 约翰·梅纳德·凯恩斯 / John Maynard Keynes
`经济 / Economic`

凯恩斯将国家干预立为提振总需求的救世方；在危机时刻充当应急止血贴，却把资本消耗与通胀常态化为长期施政惯性。

Keynes formulated state intervention as the cure for aggregate demand shortfalls; effective as a crisis patch, his doctrine normalizes permanent capital consumption and inflation.

→ [Public Spending Does Not Create Capital](../../posts/public-spending-does-not-create-capital/)

### 约翰·斯坦贝克 / John Steinbeck
`哲学 / Philosophical`

斯坦贝克把自由探索的心智视为最可贵，这一点成立；但号召对抗观念与政府，预设了它们从心智外部施力，而它们只是心智留下又遇见的残余。

Steinbeck rightly names the free exploring mind as most valuable, yet his call to fight ideas and governments presumes they act from outside the mind; they remain residue minds produce and meet.

→ [The Free Mind Is Displaced by What Claims to Protect It](../../posts/the-free-mind-is-displaced-by-what-claims-to-protect-it/)

### 约翰·洛克 / John Locke
`哲学 / Philosophical`

洛克指出了诉诸无知作为辩论启发式的特征；这在封闭检验程序中能辅助权衡置信度，但若将其误用为衡量通用心智的标尺，便将局域规则偷换为了不可通约的主观偏好。

Locke identified the argument from ignorance as a distinct heuristic; while valuable for weighing confidence within bounded protocols, deploying it to evaluate general mind confuses a local heuristic with an external ontological proof.

→ [远方的轮廓与无处之境的清单 / The Distant Silhouette and the Inventory from Nowhere](../../posts/the-distant-silhouette-and-the-inventory-from-nowhere/)

### 约翰·冯·诺依曼 / John von Neumann
`数学 / Mathematical`

冯·诺依曼主张核先发打击的论证从模型严密地推出，在展示形式推理能走多远时有用；它在此到了边界：接收这些论证的决策者觉得陌生并拒绝了它，一个心智认定为理性的东西，到达另一个心智时已经被改写。

Von Neumann's case for a nuclear first strike followed rigorously from the model, which is of use in showing how far formal reasoning can run; it reaches its boundary in that the decision-makers who received it found it alien and refused it, so what one mind registers as rational arrives in another already rewritten.

→ [理性从不跨越心智 / Rationality Never Travels Across the Mind](../../posts/rationality-never-travels-across-the-mind/)

### 卡尔·波普尔 / Karl Popper
`哲学 / Philosophical`

波普尔关于苦乐不对称的论述以“我相信”开头，并承认这并非理性论证所能裁决；后续批判方法运行在这一刀切开的场地内，无法为它作证。

Popper's asymmetry of suffering and happiness opens with 'I believe' and admits it is not rationally decidable; later critical method operates inside that founding cut and cannot certify it.

→ [The Initial Distinction, where the rationalism attempted to and failed to escape irrationality](../../posts/the-initial-distinction/) · [从形式迷思到逻辑闭环 / From the Myth of Form to the Closed Loop of Logic](../../posts/cong-xing-shi-mi-si-dao-luo-ji-bi-huan/)

### 卡尔·魏尔施特拉斯 / Karl Weierstrass
`数学 / Mathematical`

魏尔施特拉斯用极限严格奠基了微积分并构造出处处不可微函数；有力证明了形式上的连续并不保障现实直觉中的平滑。

Weierstrass formalized rigorous limits and discovered continuous nowhere-differentiable functions; his monsters proved mathematical continuity does not imply intuitive physical smoothness.

→ [连续性作为建模的权宜之计 / The Continuum is a Modeling Convenience](../../posts/the-continuum-is-a-modeling-convenience/)

### 凯文·凯利 / Kevin Kelly
`技术 / Technological`

凯利梳理了网络时代超越免费的价值驱动；富有前瞻力，但若把清单当作万能公式套用，则易失去鲜活性。

Kelly catalogues post-scarcity value drivers in networked economics; visionary for internet dynamics, his taxonomy risks becoming formulaic when applied without live context.

→ [Better Than Free After the Checklist Closes](../../posts/better-than-free-after-the-checklist-closes/)

### 库尔特·哥德尔 / Kurt Gödel
`数学 / Mathematical`

哥德尔击碎了希尔伯特封闭公理体系的企图；以严密证明确立了形式系统的边界，证实真理超越了机械证明。

Gödel shattered Hilbert's dream of a closed mathematical foundation; his work rigorously defines formal limits, confirming that truth outstrips mechanical theorem proving.

→ [No System Can Be Kept Closed](../../posts/no-system-can-be-kept-closed/)

### 老子 / Laozi
`哲学 / Philosophical`

老子将造化运化归于无为自然；极利于破除执念妄作，但若抽离实践辨析，也容易流于消极顺服。

Laozi locates cosmic dynamics in spontaneous non-action; profound for relinquishing grasping, it is easily co-opted into fatalistic passivity when stripped of rigour.

→ [有无之边](../../posts/you-wu-zhi-bian/)

### 路德维希·冯·米塞斯 / Ludwig von Mises
`经济 / Economic`

米塞斯从人的行动公理推导经济学；有力瓦解了计划经济谬误，但在强权凌驾市场逻辑处解释力受限。

Mises deduces economics from human action axioms; brilliant for dismantling socialist calculation, it struggles where non-rational power asserts itself over market logic.

→ [Mises on the State: Ontological Clarity Analysis](../../posts/mises-state-analysis/)

### 路德维希·维特根斯坦 / Ludwig Wittgenstein
`哲学 / Philosophical`

“哲学问题即语言问题”的立场把语言当作世界；意义产生于意识对语言的解读，语言框架是有用的仪器，却不是判定异同的地基。

The stance that philosophy's problems are language problems treats language as world; meaning arises in consciousness interpreting language, so the language frame is a useful instrument, not the ground of difference.

→ [读《中文哲学简史》有感](../../posts/du-zhongwen-zhexue-jianshi-you-gan/)

### 马丁·海德格尔 / Martin Heidegger
`哲学 / Philosophical`

海德格尔深刻揭示了上手工具的活态沉浸优先于抽离审视的现成客体；当认知系统试图把第一人称的情感体验当成货架上的静态库存核验调取时失效；在破除笛卡尔式主客二元割裂与恢复具身存有方面具有基底意义。

Heidegger demonstrated that embodied readiness-to-hand precedes detached presence-at-hand; fails when cognition treats affective realization as an isolated inventory item to be audited on command; remains foundational in dissolving Cartesian subject-object dualism and recovering embodied presence.

→ [现成接触的因果先验与期望感受的倒置遮蔽 / Present Contact as Causal Prior and the Obscuration of Desired Feeling](../../posts/present-contact-and-the-obscuration-of-desired-feeling/)

### 玛乔丽·麦克谢恩 / Marjorie McShane
`技术 / Technological`

麦克谢恩将智能体认知奠基于显式语言微理论；利于可解释解析，却受制于手工规则对连续推理的覆盖边界。

McShane grounds agent cognition in explicit linguistic microtheories; valuable for inspectable parsing, it stops where continuous inference outstrips hand-crafted rules.

→ [Agents in the Long Game of AI — Through the Ontological Clarity Framework](../../posts/agents_leia_ontological_clarity/)

### 迈克尔·莱文 / Michael Levin
`科学 / Scientific`

莱文揭示了跨越尺度的生物电模式与形态调控；开辟了再生医学新境，但其潜空间渗入模型依然试图从客体视角推导主体。

Levin demonstrated developmental bioelectric agency across scales; groundbreaking for regenerative medicine, his model of agency ingression still assumes an externalized mechanistic frame.

→ [主体性不曾涌现 / Agency Does Not Arise](../../posts/agency-does-not-arise/)

### 纳西姆·尼古拉斯·塔勒布 / Nassim Nicholas Taleb
`哲学 / Philosophical`

塔勒布将不确定性下的凸性收益形式化；利于风险工程，但将凸性启发式生硬套入精密领域则显粗糙。

Taleb formalizes convex payoffs under uncertainty; invaluable for risk engineering, his polemical posture overextends convex heuristics into domains requiring precision.

→ [A New Deeper Look at Antifragility: Prosthesis, Not Driver](../../posts/a-new-deeper-look-at-antifragility/)

### 纳瓦尔·拉维肯特 / Naval Ravikant
`成功学 / Self-Help`

纳瓦尔把非二元哲学与现代的杠杆学说结合起来，强调空着的日程与最少的承诺，在帮助个人安顿、留出可选性时有用；它在此到了边界：把分离当作病，否认了区分在运作中的必要，而永久停在空的那一面，本身也成了一次未经审视的预测。

Naval blends Advaita non-duality with a modern account of leverage and stresses the empty calendar and minimal commitments, which is of use for personal peace and for keeping optionality; it reaches its boundary in that treating separation as a disease denies the working need for distinction, and staying permanently on the empty face becomes an unexamined prediction.

→ [Separation Isn't the Disease](../../posts/naval-separation-analysis/) · [不预测是没有觉察的预测 / Not Predicting Is Predicting Without Awareness](../../posts/not-predicting-is-predicting-without-awareness/)

### 尼古拉·尚福尔 / Nicolas Chamfort
`哲学 / Philosophical`

尚福尔以警句揭示幸福在外部不可得而在内部难寻的不对称性；准确指出了客体无法替代第一人称体认，却将寻觅语法的客体化分裂归因为实存之难。

Chamfort captures the asymmetry that happiness cannot be found externally and remains difficult within; invaluable for exposing the failure of external objects, it mistakes the objectifying fracture of searching for an ontological friction.

→ [寻觅的语法与淡出的观察者 / The Grammar of Finding and the Fading Observer](../../posts/the-grammar-of-finding-and-the-fading-observer/)

### 保罗·丘奇兰德 / Paul Churchland
`哲学 / Philosophical`

丘奇兰德的取消式唯物主义认为常识心理学是一套不完善的理论，其所指的第一人称现象会随神经科学成熟而被取消；它在用第一人称的理智去否认第一人称的理智之处到了边界，作为让神经科学不必迁就日常用语切分大脑的提醒，它依然有用。

Churchland's eliminative materialism holds folk psychology to be an imperfect theory whose first-person referents will be eliminated as neuroscience matures; it reaches its boundary where the first-person intellect is used to deny the first-person intellect, while as a reminder that neuroscience need not carve the brain to fit everyday speech it stays useful.

→ [无法闭合的痕迹 / The Unclosable Trace](../../posts/the-unclosable-trace/)

### 皮埃尔-西蒙·拉普拉斯 / Pierre-Simon Laplace
`科学 / Scientific`

拉普拉斯把天体力学与概率论体系化，设想一个知道全部粒子位置与作用力的智能能算出整个未来；这幅图在计算者自身的位置上到了边界，因为把宇宙算完要求站到宇宙之外，作为提前算准行星位置、整理不确定性的工具，它依然有用。

Laplace systematized celestial mechanics and probability and imagined an intelligence that, knowing all particle positions and forces, could calculate the whole future; the picture reaches its boundary at the calculator's own position, since calculating the universe to the end requires standing outside it, while as a tool for calculating planetary positions ahead and organizing uncertainty it stays useful.

→ [决定论机器 / The Deterministic Machine](../../posts/the-deterministic-machine/) · [没有固定者的固定 / Fixed by Nothing](../../posts/fixed-by-nothing/)

### 乔治·斯宾塞-布朗 / G. Spencer-Brown
`数学 / Mathematical`

斯宾塞-布朗的形式之律确立了一切系统起源于划出一道区分的原初行动；当形式体系遗忘区分必须由具体中心在当下划出、转而将区分沉淀物当成自足客观法则时失效；在形式化奠基第一人称创生世界与自指递归上具有不可替代的公理价值。

Spencer-Brown's Laws of Form established that every formal universe originates in the primordial act of drawing a distinction; fails when cognition forgets distinctions are drawn by situated centers and reifies marks into autonomous cosmic laws; remains irreplaceable in formalizing self-referential closure and the first-person origin of form.

→ [现成接触的因果先验与期望感受的倒置遮蔽 / Present Contact as Causal Prior and the Obscuration of Desired Feeling](../../posts/present-contact-and-the-obscuration-of-desired-feeling/)

### 瑞·达利欧 / Ray Dalio
`成功学 / Self-Help`

达利欧的《原则》假定流程与创意择优驱动结果；它事后贴合桥水的历史，作为机构化编码有用，早期优势却来自这套教条无法迁移的判断力。

Dalio's Principles assume process and idea meritocracy drive results; they fit Bridgewater's record retrospectively, useful as institutional codification, while early edge came from judgment the doctrine cannot transfer.

→ [成功学: Theories After Success, Mistaken for Theories Leading to Success](../../posts/cheng-gong-xue/)

### 雷·库兹韦尔 / Ray Kurzweil
`技术 / Technological`

库兹韦尔将指数律视为历史的第一推动力；便于跟踪硬件密度，却混淆了载体算力与心智主体。

Kurzweil posits exponential law as history's primary driver; useful for tracking hardware density, it conflates substrate acceleration with conscious agency.

→ [The Exponential and the Linear Eye](../../posts/Analysis_Adolescence_of_Technology/)

### 勒内·笛卡尔 / René Descartes
`哲学 / Philosophical`

笛卡尔将认识论确定性锚定于第一人称怀疑；开创了理性主义体系，其心物二分却为后世把心智还原为发条机械埋下诱因。

Descartes grounded epistemological certainty in first-person cogito while separating mind from extension; foundational for modern science, his split led to mechanistic reductions of mind.

→ [笛卡尔的认识论跃迁与造神狂热 / The Cartesian Epistemic Leap and the Frenzy of Creation](../../posts/the-cartesian-overstep-and-the-frenzy-of-creation/) · [无法闭合的痕迹 / The Unclosable Trace](../../posts/the-unclosable-trace/)

### 里克·鲁宾 / Rick Rubin
`成功学 / Self-Help`

鲁宾将艺术创作视作校准天线接收宇宙信号；极利于释放创作直觉，却弱化了技术基本功与剪裁推敲。

Rubin treats artistic creation as tuning into cosmic resonance; wonderful for unblocking creative intuition, it underplays technical craftsmanship and editing rigor.

→ [The Interesting Case of Rubin](../../posts/the-interesting-case-of-rubin/)

### 罗杰·彭罗斯 / Roger Penrose
`科学 / Scientific`

彭罗斯从人对哥德尔定理的理解推出意识不可计算；该推论预设了以单一系统看待心智，没有触及证明在主体之间的界限。

Penrose infers non-computable consciousness from human understanding of Gödel; the inference presumes a single-system framing of mind, leaving untouched the intersubjective limit of proof.

→ [On Closed Systems, Open Minds, and the Limits of Proof](../../posts/on-closed-systems-open-minds-and-the-limits-of-proof/) · [另一种相干 / The Other Coherence](../../posts/the-other-coherence/)

### 罗宾·汉森 / Robin Hanson
`科学 / Scientific`

汉森提出大过滤器假说以解释深空寂静与星际扩张门槛；当其将深空无声归因于客观进化漏斗并反向推导人类文明的集体安逸时失效，因为文明并非具备选择意志的决策实体；在估算技术跃迁的宏观概率分布方面保持有用。

Hanson formulated the Great Filter hypothesis to account for cosmic silence and interstellar expansion thresholds; fails when attributing deep-space silence to an objective evolutionary sieve and diagnosing collective civilizational complacency, because a civilization possesses no sovereign agency; remains useful for modeling probabilistic milestones across macro-scale technological transitions.

→ [费米悖论、主体性错配与大过滤器的投射 / The Fermi Paradox, Reified Agency, and the Projection of the Great Filter](../../posts/the-fermi-paradox-reified-agency-and-the-great-filter/)

### 萨宾·霍森菲尔德 / Sabine Hossenfelder
`科学 / Scientific`

霍森菲尔德认为理解哥德尔是可形式化的元推理，无需不可计算性；在其前提内成立，却只处理单一系统能否走出封闭。

Hossenfelder holds understanding Gödel is formalizable meta-reasoning, so needs no non-computability; valid within her premise, it still addresses only whether a single system steps outside closure.

→ [On Closed Systems, Open Minds, and the Limits of Proof](../../posts/on-closed-systems-open-minds-and-the-limits-of-proof/)

### 史蒂夫·乔布斯 / Steve Jobs
`技术 / Technological`

乔布斯以不妥协的苛求击碎因循守旧的行业排期；有力激发出产品极致，但将其神化却容易使人忽视技术约束的硬度。

Jobs demanded product perfection by rejecting conventional industry timelines; inspiring for product craft, mythologizing his methods blinds followers to technical realities.

→ [The Reality Distortion Field Names the Closed Map](../../posts/the-reality-distortion-field-names-the-closed-map/)

### 斯图尔特·哈梅罗夫 / Stuart Hameroff
`科学 / Scientific`

哈梅罗夫把理解视为规则之外的行动，由量子客观还原提供；在该本体论下自洽，却止步于单个心智，不问开放如何向另一心智证明。

Hameroff treats understanding as an act outside rules, supplied by quantum objective reduction; coherent given that ontology, it stops at one mind and never asks how openness is demonstrated to another.

→ [On Closed Systems, Open Minds, and the Limits of Proof](../../posts/on-closed-systems-open-minds-and-the-limits-of-proof/) · [另一种相干 / The Other Coherence](../../posts/the-other-coherence/)

### 托马斯·内格尔 / Thomas Nagel
`哲学 / Philosophical`

内格尔深刻指出了主观质感的不可还原与无处之境的幻妄；有力揭露了物理主义的自傲，其论述深邃而富于警示。

Nagel analyzed the irreducibility of subjective consciousness and the illusion of a view from nowhere; brilliant for exposing physicalist limits, his framework remains purely analytical.

→ [隐秘的上帝之眼 / The Invisible God's Eye](../../posts/the-invisible-gods-eye/) · [远方的轮廓与无处之境的清单 / The Distant Silhouette and the Inventory from Nowhere](../../posts/the-distant-silhouette-and-the-inventory-from-nowhere/) · [框架的盲区 / The Blind Spot of the Frame](../../posts/the-blind-spot-of-the-frame/)

### 托马斯·索维尔 / Thomas Sowell
`经济 / Economic`

索维尔主张以激励而非目标评判政策，这一观察准确；但它仍预设可从豁免的旁观位置给出系统纠正，未能返回第一人称并意识到第一人称视角的不可脱离性。

Sowell's point that policies should be judged by incentives, not goals, is accurate; it still presumes a systematic corrective from an exempted observer stand, failing to return to the inescapable first-person perspective.

→ [索维尔的表象洞察与根因之偏 / Sowell Observed the Surface Problem](../../posts/sowell-observed-the-surface-problem/) · [未经自我审视的批判者 / The Unexamined Critic](../../posts/the-unexamined-critic/)

### 王阳明 / Wang Yangming
`哲学 / Philosophical`

王阳明格竹七日而病，由此离开朱熹的即物穷理、走向知行合一，这一转向有用；把格竹的失败读成格物本身走不通，在此到了边界：失败出在把必须在第一人称之内展开的微观因果推演，做成了第三人称的凝视。

Wang Yangming investigated the bamboo for seven days, fell ill, and so left Zhu Xi's exhausting of principle in things for the unity of knowing and acting, a turn of real use; reading the failure as proof that investigating things cannot work is where it reaches its boundary, since the failure lay in making a micro causal reasoning that must unfold within the first person into a third-person gaze.

→ [格物致知与信息复杂度 / Investigating Things and the Nature of Information Complexity](../../posts/ge-wu-zhi-zhi-yu-xin-xi-fu-za-du-de-ben-zhi/)

### 维尔纳·海森堡 / Werner Heisenberg
`科学 / Scientific`

海森堡的测不准原理界定了微观共轭变量同时测量的精度极限，为量子理论立下基石；借它作比去读个人的选择，在此到了边界：测不准说的是观察者站的位置取不全变量，而选择在第一人称之内是确定而自洽的，外部测到的随机，正是内部主权的投影。

Heisenberg's uncertainty principle bounds the precision with which conjugate micro variables can be measured together, a cornerstone of quantum theory; borrowed as a figure for individual choice, it reaches its boundary in that uncertainty names the observer's position, from which the variables cannot all be had, while choice within the first person is determinate and consistent, and the randomness measured outside is the projection of inner sovereignty.

→ [自由的测不准 / The Uncertainty of Freedom](../../posts/zi-you-de-ce-bu-zhun/)

### 威廉·诺德豪斯 / William Nordhaus
`经济 / Economic`

诺德豪斯度量了战后非农经济中技术变迁的剩余分配，记录下生产者仅捕获微小私人回报而绝大部分耗散为社会剩余的宏观轨迹；该模型提供了高精度的经验遥测，但统计度量印证而非构筑了因果律，未能将其溯源至行动先于结果的不可还原先验。

Nordhaus measured the surplus distribution of technological change in the postwar nonfarm economy, recording how tiny private returns dissipate into massive social dividends; providing high-resolution empirical telemetry, this econometric model validates rather than grounds causality, stopping short of tracing the sequence back to the irreducible prior of action.

→ [私人残差与社会剩余的因果次序 / The Causal Order of Private Residuals and Social Surplus](../../posts/the-causal-order-of-private-residuals-and-social-surplus/) · [百万亿地平线之外 / Beyond the Hundred-Trillion Horizon](../../posts/beyond-the-hundred-trillion-horizon/)

### 自由意志的必然涌现假说 / The Inevitable Emergence Hypothesis of Free Will
`科学 / Scientific`

该假说预设客观复杂度的攀升能够自发催生主观能动性，试图在因果链条中为意志安放输出接口；这一假定在第三人称模型中回溯性推演意志时陷入了将画中之眼归于画作本身的偷换，却在刻画认知系统算力升级与行为复杂度的经验关联上提供了有效的工程建模工具。

The hypothesis assumes escalating complexity spontaneously generates subjective agency, seeking to locate an output interface for volition within causal chains; this premise commits a category error by retrospectively deriving the painting's eye from the canvas itself, yet provides an effective engineering modeling framework for mapping computational scaling to behavioral complexity.

→ [框架的盲区 / The Blind Spot of the Frame](../../posts/the-blind-spot-of-the-frame/)

### 激活叠加与表征向量控制 / Activation Addition and Steering Vectors
`科学 / Scientific`

机械可解释性研究以为找到特定激活向量便锁定了意识；控制表征向量调控的只是输出词元的统计倾向，与心智的活生生体验风马牛不相及。

Mechanistic interpretability treats finding activation vectors as locating consciousness; steering directions adjust statistical word frequencies, not living subjective experience.

→ [The Real Lesson from the Consciousness Vector Paper](../../posts/the-real-lesson-from-the-consciousness-vector-paper/)

### 高级苦假说 / Advanced Pain Hypothesis
`成功学 / Self-Help`

将少数人因发现认知净收益而自发持续的行动事后重述为对高级痛苦的特殊耐受天赋；遗漏了单向受苦在神经与代谢上的不可持续性，在作为心理免责与放弃借口时保持心理防御功能。

Restates sustained action driven by discovered cognitive surplus as a rare innate endowment for enduring higher-order pain; it ignores that uncompensated suffering is biologically and neurologically unsustainable, functioning solely as a defensive alibi for quitting.

→ [观察的因果僭越与第一人称的收益差 / The Causal Overstep of Observation and the First-Person Surplus](../../posts/the-causal-overstep-of-observation-and-the-first-person-surplus/)

### 人工智能存在性风险 / AI Existential Risk (x-risk)
`技术 / Technological`

AI灭绝论将超智能视作失控的宇宙事件；虽有警示功用，却抽离了当务之急的商业垄断与治理问题。

AI extinction arguments treat superintelligence as an uncontrollable cosmic event; effective for safety vigilance, they displace near-term commercial control questions.

→ [The Collapsed Debate](../../posts/the-collapsed-debate/)

### 算法过拟合 / Algorithmic Overfitting
`科学 / Scientific`

过拟合将密集记忆训练集视同掌握了通用规律；对历史分布的高度贴合，反而削弱了应对前沿变动的应变力。

Overfitting treats dense memorization of training sets as generalized wisdom; high fidelity to past distributions collapses predictive capability on shifting frontiers.

→ [Overfitting as the Follower Mode](../../posts/overfitting-as-the-follower-mode/)

### 无我 / Anattā (Non-Self)
`宗教 / Religious`

佛教的无我看清自我形象是构造出来的、而非终极的根基，执着于它是苦的根源；当它被读成要消灭自我、根除欲望、让能动消融于虚空时到了边界，佛教自身也拒绝这种断灭见，作为松开对冻结形象之执着的洞察，它依然锋利。

Buddhist non-self sees that the self-image is constructed rather than the final ground, and that clinging to it is the root of suffering; it reaches its boundary when read as a demand to extinguish the self, uproot desire and dissolve agency into a void, a reading Buddhism itself rejects as annihilationism, while as an insight that loosens clinging to a frozen image it stays sharp.

→ [心智的几何学 / The Geometry of Mind](../../posts/the-geometry-of-mind/)

### 万物有灵论 / Animism
`宗教 / Religious`

万物有灵论把意识归于河流、山岳与森林；它切身登记到了自然中自我调节的回路，却在把人的形象，愤怒、报复、虚荣，投射到这些回路上时到了边界，作为对回路折回自身的那种回响的感受，它依然是一个出发点。

Animism attributes consciousness to rivers, mountains and forests; it registers, directly, the self-regulating loops of nature, and reaches its boundary when it projects the human image, anger, vengeance, vanity, onto those loops, while as a feel for the echo of loops folding back on themselves it remains a starting point.

→ [折叠的连续谱 / The Continuum of the Fold](../../posts/the-continuum-of-the-fold/)

### 反脆弱理论 / Antifragility
`经济 / Economic`

反脆弱论主张系统能从冲击中获益；在可承受阈值内有效，一旦强冲击击穿系统底座，反脆弱便难以为继。

Antifragility posits systems benefit from shock and volatility; this holds within survivable thresholds, but collapses when structural shocks destroy the coordinating frame.

→ [A New Deeper Look at Antifragility: Prosthesis, Not Driver](../../posts/a-new-deeper-look-at-antifragility/)

### 大脑反向传播假说 / Backpropagation in the Brain
`科学 / Scientific`

反向传播假说假定大脑逆向回传全局误差梯度；生物神经网络依靠的是局部的正向突触可塑性，不存在对称反传机制。

Backpropagation posits biological learning computes global error gradients backward; brain tissue operates via forward local plasticity without symmetric weight transport.

→ [The Brain Does Not Backprop, but Propagates](../../posts/the-brain-does-not-backprop/)

### 大过滤器假说 / The Great Filter Hypothesis
`科学 / Scientific`

大过滤器假说预设从原始生命到星际扩张之间存在一道或多道极难跨越的统计门槛，并将深空寂静解释为文明面临的客观演化漏斗；当其将深空的沉寂反向推导为人类社会作为一个整体在安逸中走向衰竭时失效，因为文明并不具备选择与行动的主体意志，宏观分布只是无数微观个体局部权衡的统计沉淀；在测算技术演进的宏观阶段与多步转移概率时保持有效。

The Great Filter hypothesis posits one or more extraordinarily improbable evolutionary barriers between prebiotic chemistry and durable interstellar expansion, interpreting cosmic silence as an objective civilizational sieve; it fails when inverting the silence into a diagnosis of collective human complacency, because a civilization possesses no unified decision-making agency and macroscopic outcomes are mere statistical aggregates of discrete individual tradeoffs; it remains useful for categorizing macroscopic technological milestones and transitional probabilities.

→ [费米悖论、主体性错配与大过滤器的投射 / The Fermi Paradox, Reified Agency, and the Projection of the Great Filter](../../posts/the-fermi-paradox-reified-agency-and-the-great-filter/)

### 行为经济学 / Behavioral Economics
`经济 / Economic`

行为经济学把损失厌恶、概率加权等偏离与环境作为修正加进理性选择装置，在让对既往选择的重构更细致时有用；它在此到了边界：先验被修正而从未被挑战，共享的形式装置为选择奠基这一核心前提被保留下来，可携带的演算仍被当作对能动的描述。

Behavioral economics adds departures such as loss aversion and probability weighting, and environments, as revisions to the rational-choice apparatus, which is of use in densifying the reconstruction of past choices; it reaches its boundary in that the prior is conditioned and never challenged, keeping the core premise that a shared formal apparatus grounds choice, so the portable calculus still stands as the description of agency.

→ [The Rational Choice Postulate Is the Original Sin of All the Wrongs in Modern Economics Theories](../../posts/the-rational-choice-postulate-is-the-original-sin/) · [理性从不跨越心智 / Rationality Never Travels Across the Mind](../../posts/rationality-never-travels-across-the-mind/)

### 生物基因决定论 / Biological Determinism
`科学 / Scientific`

把人的行为归于基因、激素与神经回路，在描述选择所受的身体约束时有用；它在此到了边界：生物学的测量是观察者地平线内的相关与约束，不是选择的发起者，约束界定的是选项的菜单，不是选择者，把青少年的决定归于大脑发育，替父母开脱，也冻结了年轻人自己的学习回路。

Attributing human behaviour to genes, hormones and neural circuitry is of use in describing the bodily constraints on choice; it reaches its boundary in that biological measurements are correlations and constraints within an observer's horizon, not the originator of the choice, constraints define the menu of options rather than the chooser, and crediting adolescents' decisions to brain development excuses parents while freezing young people's own learning loop.

→ [引导不是占有 / Guidance Is Not Ownership](../../posts/guidance-is-not-ownership/)

### 比特币作为价值储存 / Bitcoin as Store of Value
`经济 / Economic`

比特币起初是一件让人在法币体系之外表达能动性的开放媒介，作为交换的工具有用；把价值归给代币本身、把它当作静态的价值储存，在此到了边界，媒介冻结了资本，转成零和的投机，又靠法币支撑的稳定币补上交易用途。

Bitcoin began as an open medium for expressing agency outside the fiat system, of use as an instrument of exchange; crediting value to the token itself and treating it as a static store of value is where it reaches its boundary, as the medium freezes capital, turns into zero-sum speculation, and leans on fiat-backed stablecoins to patch its transactional use.

→ [自我价值、反向财富转移与过去成功的陷阱 / Self-Worthiness, Reverse Wealth Transfer, and the Trap of Past Success](../../posts/self-worthiness-reverse-wealth-transfer-and-the-trap-of-past-success/)

### 块状宇宙世界线理论 / Block Universe Worldline
`科学 / Scientific`

假定四维时空中所有历史与未来皆并存不移；在广义相对论几何中有用，却把在当下绘就的时空全景图误认为了实在本体。

Presumes spacetime is an eternal four-dimensional block where past, present, and future coexist; mathematically powerful in relativity, it mistakes a static map for unfolding time.

→ [四维长鹿的幻象与当下的模型 / The Myth of the Four-Dimensional Deer and the Present Model](../../posts/the-myth-of-the-four-dimensional-deer-and-the-present-model/) · [心智的几何学 / The Geometry of Mind](../../posts/the-geometry-of-mind/) · [时间是因果的不可逆方向与离散步长 / Time Is the Irreversible Direction and Discrete Step of Causality](../../posts/time-is-causality-not-a-dimension/)

### 古典功利主义 / Classical Utilitarianism
`哲学 / Philosophical`

最大幸福原则把幸福当作可跨人加总的量；波普尔的修正保留了同一外部供给前提，只是调换了计入的一方。

The greatest happiness principle treats happiness as an addable quantity across persons; Popper's correction keeps the same external-supply premise and only reverses which side counts.

→ [The Initial Distinction, where the rationalism attempted to and failed to escape irrationality](../../posts/the-initial-distinction/)

### 集体无意识 / Collective Unconscious
`哲学 / Philosophical`

集体无意识预设一种先于个人的深层结构，在描述许多人共有的倾向时有用；它在此到了边界：追溯下去，它是交出更新与代为决定相互对接、在许多心智间稳定之后留下的共振痕迹，机制落在每个心智是否把误差传回自己可更新的参数。

The collective unconscious presumes a deep structure prior to individuals, which is of use in describing tendencies many people share; it reaches its boundary in that, traced back, it is the resonance left when handing over updates meets complementary deciding-for-others and stabilizes across many minds, while the mechanism rests on whether each mind carries the error back to its own updatable parameters.

→ [人生是一个持续的自我迭代过程 / Life as a Continuous Process of Self-Iteration](../../posts/ren-sheng-shi-yi-ge-chi-xu-de-zi-wo-die-dai-guo-cheng/)

### 无理解的能力 / Competence Without Comprehension
`哲学 / Philosophical`

主张复杂功能实现可以脱离内在理解；对自动化机制有效，却错把下游产出的符号痕迹当成了拥有自觉的心智。

Claims complex functional competence naturally supersedes conscious comprehension; useful for automated systems, it mistakes output tokens for an experiencing mind.

→ [大倒置的消解 / The Dissolution of the Great Reversal](../../posts/the-dissolution-of-the-great-reversal/)

### 宪政人工智能 / Constitutional AI
`技术 / Technological`

宪政AI预设伦理可通过声明性原则工程化；对安全防护有效，却把心智的主权道德决断外化为静态条文。

Constitutional AI presumes ethics can be operationalized via declarative principles; effective for safety guardrails, it externalizes sovereign moral judgment into static text.

→ [The Projection Called Claude: An Ontological Analysis of Anthropic's Constitution](../../posts/claudes-constitution-analysis/)

### 批判理性主义 / Critical Rationalism
`哲学 / Philosophical`

批判性排除能抛弃从某一区分推出的东西，却无法消解使排除成为可能的那个区分；理性始于先于理性的一刀，那一刀是它的条件。

Critical elimination can discard what follows from a distinction but cannot dissolve the distinction that made elimination possible; rationality begins from a pre-rational cut that conditions it.

→ [The Initial Distinction, where the rationalism attempted to and failed to escape irrationality](../../posts/the-initial-distinction/)

### 多巴胺驱动目的论 / Dopamine-Driven Teleology
`科学 / Scientific`

神经生物学框架将多巴胺视作成功的因果源头；递质登记代谢就绪度，价值选择却来自心智意图。

Neurobiological framing treats dopamine as the causal origin of achievement; neurochemistry registers metabolic readiness, whereas value choice belongs to living intent.

→ [Winners/Losers: The Transmutation Frame](../../posts/huberman-winners-losers/)

### 达克效应反转 / Dunning-Kruger Inversion
`科学 / Scientific`

达克效应跟踪低认知者的自大；而在高智商场景，智力会编织精巧说辞为盲点辩护，更难破除自身盲视。

The Dunning-Kruger framework tracks incompetence overconfidence; at high capability, intelligence constructs sophisticated rationalizations, making internal errors harder to pierce.

→ [Intelligence Is the Thing Most Likely to Trap Itself](../../posts/intelligence-traps-itself/)

### 达克效应 / Dunning–Kruger Effect
`科学 / Scientific`

被当作自我觉察阶梯使用时，这一效应成了高阶标签，可让使用者免于真正触碰边界；谦逊的修辞替代了实际的劳作。

Invoked as a ladder of self-awareness, the effect becomes a higher-order label that can exempt its user from contact with an actual boundary; rhetoric of humility substitutes for the work.

→ [“知道自己不知道”是一种修辞假象 / Knowing That You Don't Know Is a Rhetorical Illusion](../../posts/zhi-dao-zi-ji-bu-zhi-dao-de-ren-zhi-jia-xiang/)

### 意识涌现论 / Emergence of Consciousness
`科学 / Scientific`

假定主观体验能由无意识的物质复杂排列自发产生；推动了神经科学探索，却在范畴上试图用客体现象推导观察者本身。

Presumes conscious awareness arises mechanistically from non-conscious material interactions; helpful for cognitive neuroscience, it commits a category leap by trying to generate the subject.

→ [主体性不曾涌现 / Agency Does Not Arise](../../posts/agency-does-not-arise/)

### 熵引力 / Entropic Gravity
`科学 / Scientific`

从熵或量子信息推导引力，把质量读作集中到足以登记为物质的信息，把引力读作这种集中的统计趋势，在复现熟悉的极限、重新诠释引力的微弱时有用；它在此到了边界：它仍是一次选择，舍弃了一份无穷的细节余量，它的临时封闭性可用，却不免于重新诠释。

Deriving gravity from entropy or quantum information reads mass as information concentrated enough to register as matter and gravity as a statistical tendency of that concentration, which is of use in recovering familiar limits and reinterpreting gravity's weakness; it reaches its boundary in that it remains a selection that discards an unbounded remainder of detail, its temporary closure usable but not exempt from reinterpretation.

→ [选择、矛盾与开放的现实 / Selection, Contradiction, and Open Reality](../../posts/selection-contradiction-and-open-reality/)

### 期望效用理论 / Expected Utility Theory
`经济 / Economic`

期望效用理论把一套一致的偏好结构当作可以在人与人之间搬运，在一场具体的交换里整理选择时有用；它在此到了边界：每个心智都在本地重新诠释它，它只在特定的交换里好用，而不是一条可以转移的论断。

Expected utility theory treats one coherent preference structure as interpersonally portable, which is of use in organizing choices inside a particular exchange; it reaches its boundary in that each mind reinterprets it locally, so it serves inside particular exchanges rather than as a transferable thesis.

→ [理性从不跨越心智 / Rationality Never Travels Across the Mind](../../posts/rationality-never-travels-across-the-mind/)

### 延展心智理论 / Extended Mind Thesis
`哲学 / Philosophical`

延展心智理论主张外部工具构成心智的一部分；适用于分析计算支架，却模糊了第一人称觉知的主体界限。

Extended cognition argues external artifacts form part of the mind; useful for analyzing computational scaffolds, it blurs the boundary of conscious first-person awareness.

→ [Tools as Active Extensions of Cognition](../../posts/extended-cognition-refined/) · [维度即投影 / Dimensions Are Projections](../../posts/dimensions-are-projections/)

### 证伪主义 / Falsificationism
`哲学 / Philosophical`

证伪常被误用为把反面证据解释掉或靠驳倒对方证明自己；每次检验都晚于其针对的过程一步，方法本身因此可修正，而非结案工具。

Falsification is misused either to explain away counterevidence or to refute opponents as self-proof; each test lags the process it targets, so the method itself stays revisable rather than a closing tool.

→ [关于归因：从事实到因果，从外部节点到内部迭代](../../posts/guan-yu-gui-yin/) · [从形式迷思到逻辑闭环 / From the Myth of Form to the Closed Loop of Logic](../../posts/cong-xing-shi-mi-si-dao-luo-ji-bi-huan/)

### 费米悖论 / The Fermi Paradox
`科学 / Scientific`

费米悖论预设地外智能会在与人相近的时间窗口里、以按人的感官格式化的信号出现，于是追问它们为何沉默；这个预设在窗口之外到了边界，因为一个真正“外”的回路无法被认出，一旦能握手便不再是“外”，作为给搜寻一个可操作对象的问题，它依然有用。

The Fermi paradox presumes extraterrestrial intelligence would appear in a time window close to the human one, with signals formatted to human senses, and so asks why it is silent; the presumption reaches its boundary outside that window, since a truly other loop cannot be recognized and stops being other once a handshake is made, while as a question that gives the search something to operate on it stays useful.

→ [折叠的连续谱 / The Continuum of the Fold](../../posts/the-continuum-of-the-fold/) · [费米悖论、主体性错配与大过滤器的投射 / The Fermi Paradox, Reified Agency, and the Projection of the Great Filter](../../posts/the-fermi-paradox-reified-agency-and-the-great-filter/)

### 有限游戏与无限游戏 / Finite and Infinite Games
`哲学 / Philosophical`

詹姆斯·卡斯把为赢而玩、规则既定的有限游戏，与为让游戏继续而玩、边界可以扩展的无限游戏分开；这一区分在被当成两类人的标签时到了边界，因为同一个心智在不同时刻两种都在玩，作为看清地位竞赛只是一场有限游戏的工具，它依然锋利。

James Carse set finite games, played to win within fixed rules, apart from the infinite game, played to keep the play going with an extendable boundary; the distinction reaches its boundary when it is used to label two kinds of people, since the same Mind plays both at different moments, while as a tool for seeing that a status contest is only a finite game it stays sharp.

→ [可延展的地平线 / The Extendable Horizon](../../posts/the-extendable-horizon/) · [自我价值、反向财富转移与过去成功的陷阱 / Self-Worthiness, Reverse Wealth Transfer, and the Trap of Past Success](../../posts/self-worthiness-reverse-wealth-transfer-and-the-trap-of-past-success/) · [成功的几何学 / The Geometry of Success](../../posts/the-geometry-of-success/)

### 地平说认知悖论 / Flat Earth Epistemology
`意识形态 / Ideological`

阴谋论认识论能在局部经验里拼凑出自圆其说的闭环；地平模型虽不妨碍短途散步，一旦面对洲际航海便漏洞百出。

Conspiracy epistemologies assemble internally consistent arguments from local observations; flat-earth models work for local walking, yet collapse when forced to navigate global planetary navigation.

→ [路口的转向与地平说的行动悖论 / Turning at the Crossroad and the Flat-Earth Action Paradox](../../posts/lu-kou-de-zhuan-xiang-yu-di-ping-shuo-de-xing-dong-bei-lun/) · [切平面的行者与完美球面的踩空者 / The Walking Tangent and the Stumbling Sphere](../../posts/the-walking-tangent-and-the-stumbling-sphere/) · [投影的阶梯与因果的锚点 / The Ladder of Projections and the Anchor of Causality](../../posts/tou-ying-de-jie-ti-yu-yin-guo-de-mao-dian/)

### 公式化成功学教条 / Formulaic Success Dogma (Cheng Gong Xue)
`成功学 / Self-Help`

从成功者身上事后提炼公式与原则，在记录走过的路时有用；它在此到了边界：成功是在别人觉得有趣之前异步地迁入未绘图的地带，事后的公式把这次漂移读成插队或秘诀，没找到什么的那些漂移不被讲述，结局里也装着落到这个人头上的东西。

Distilling formulas and principles from the successful after the fact is of use in recording the path walked; it reaches its boundary in that success is asynchronous migration into unmapped territory before anyone finds it interesting, the post-hoc formula reads that drift as line-cutting or a secret, the drifts that found nothing are not told, and the outcome also holds what fell to the person.

→ [成功的几何学 / The Geometry of Success](../../posts/the-geometry-of-success/)

### 功能性社会层级论 / Functional Social Hierarchy
`哲学 / Philosophical`

均质化教条将一切社会层级均斥为压迫；现实中功能分工是不同个体能力与专注度差异在面对协作挑战时的自然沉淀。

Egalitarian dogma treats all organizational hierarchies as predatory impositions; functional differentiation precipitates naturally as varying individual talents align with task demands.

→ [Hierarchy from Individual Difference](../../posts/hierarchy-from-individual-difference/)

### 心灵哲学功能主义 / Functionalism (Philosophy of Mind)
`哲学 / Philosophical`

功能主义以因果角色界定心理状态，其前提是描述者可以立于被描述的心智之外，这一前提在理论被扩展到持有它的心智自身时失效，因为认真持有与还原主观性都必须占据它所抹去的观察者之位；作为描述信息处理多重实现、指导系统设计与比较的工具，它依然锋利有用。

Functionalism defines mental states by causal role on the premise that the describer can stand outside the mind described; the premise fails once the theory is extended to the mind holding it, since both serious endorsement and the reduction of subjectivity must occupy the observer position it erases, while as a tool for describing multiply realizable information processing and guiding system design it remains sharp and useful.

→ [无人能持有的功能主义 / The Functionalism No Functionalist Can Hold](../../posts/the-functionalism-no-functionalist-can-hold/) · [无法闭合的痕迹 / The Unclosable Trace](../../posts/the-unclosable-trace/)

### AGI功能主义理论 / Functionalist Theory of AGI
`技术 / Technological`

功能主义假定在行为外表上仿效人类即可确证心智成型；行为拟合测定的只是输出相似度，对主体意识的缺席全无察觉。

Functionalism presumes matching external human conversational behavior proves the arrival of mind; behavioral mimicry measures output fidelity, silent on subjective consciousness.

→ [The Presumption of AGI and the View from Outside](../../posts/the-presumption-of-agi-and-the-view-from-outside/)

### 博弈论 / Game Theory
`数学 / Mathematical`

均衡分析假定各个主体关于价值与风险的内部地图可以互换，在模型之内推出严密的结论时有用；它在此到了边界：它的建议会遇到按自己的前提重新诠释它的心智。

Equilibrium analysis assumes agents' internal maps of value and risk are interchangeable, which is of use in drawing rigorous conclusions inside the model; it reaches its boundary in that its prescriptions meet minds that re-render them under their own premises.

→ [理性从不跨越心智 / Rationality Never Travels Across the Mind](../../posts/rationality-never-travels-across-the-mind/)

### 丰裕时代的生成性价值 / Generative Values of Abundance
`经济 / Economic`

数字经济学揭示当复制品免费，价值将转移至真实性与即时性等维度；但若将这些属性机械教条化，则又沦为常规套路。

Digital economics notes free copies shift value to qualities like authenticity and immediacy; cataloguing the eight values fails once the checklist becomes another commodity.

→ [Better Than Free After the Checklist Closes](../../posts/better-than-free-after-the-checklist-closes/)

### 梯度下降法 / Gradient Descent
`技术 / Technological`

机器学习叙事将最优化人格化为探求真理的智能；梯度更新只是机械执行局部的导数步长，全无全局远见。

Machine learning narratives treat optimization as an intelligent agent seeking truth; gradient updates execute blind local derivative steps without global foresight.

→ [The Descent That Doesn't Know Where It's Going](../../posts/the-descent-that-doesnt-know-where-its-going/)

### 哥德尔不完备定理 / Gödel's Incompleteness Theorems
`数学 / Mathematical`

哥德尔证明任何包含算术的无矛盾系统都无法在内部证明自身一致；形式公理系统注定无法达致孤立自足的真理封闭。

Gödel proves any consistent formal system containing arithmetic cannot prove its own consistency; formal axiomatic structures cannot achieve closed, self-contained truth.

→ [Potential Infinity and the Temporary Closures of Mathematical Thought](../../posts/potential-infinity-and-the-temporary-closures-of-mathematical-thought/) · [On Closed Systems, Open Minds, and the Limits of Proof](../../posts/on-closed-systems-open-minds-and-the-limits-of-proof/) · [No System Can Be Kept Closed](../../posts/no-system-can-be-kept-closed/) · [寻觅的语法与淡出的观察者 / The Grammar of Finding and the Fading Observer](../../posts/the-grammar-of-finding-and-the-fading-observer/)

### 成长型思维 / Growth Mindset
`成功学 / Self-Help`

成长型思维主张能力可以通过努力增长，而不是固定的禀赋；它在被当成事后给走在前面的人贴的标签、用来排名他人时到了边界，因为结果里还有落在身上的东西，作为不把当前位置当成守恒封闭盒子的取向，它依然好用。

The growth mindset holds that ability grows through effort rather than being a fixed endowment; it reaches its boundary when used as an after-the-fact label for those ahead and a way of ranking others, since outcomes also hold what fell to someone, while as an orientation that refuses to treat the present position as a conserved, closed box it stays useful.

→ [可延展的地平线 / The Extendable Horizon](../../posts/the-extendable-horizon/)

### 哈耶克价格机制 / Hayekian Price Mechanism
`经济 / Economic`

哈耶克将市场价格视作分散知识的协调信号；价格是各方表达主观诉求的动态发声，而非静态公式能算定的恒常锚点。

Hayek frames market prices as decentralized information aggregators; prices serve as communicative signals coordinating private knowledge, rather than static equilibrium anchors.

→ [Price as Utterance, Understanding as Trade](../../posts/price-as-utterance-understanding-as-trade/)

### 海森堡测不准原理 / Heisenberg Uncertainty Principle
`科学 / Scientific`

测不准原理界定了微观共轭变量同时测量的精度极限，在量子力学里有用；借它作比，在此到了边界：个人的选择在第一人称之内是确定而自洽的，从外部却取不全变量，只能测到统计随机，内部主权越确定，外部测得的表观熵越大，测不准说的是观察的位置，不是选择的缺席。

The uncertainty principle bounds the precision with which conjugate micro variables can be measured together, of use in quantum mechanics; borrowed as a figure, it reaches its boundary in that an individual's choice is determinate and consistent within the first person while from outside its variables cannot all be had and only statistical randomness is measured, so that the more determinate the inner sovereignty, the greater the apparent entropy measured outside, uncertainty naming the observer's position, not the absence of choice.

→ [自由的测不准 / The Uncertainty of Freedom](../../posts/zi-you-de-ce-bu-zhun/)

### 希尔伯特数学大纲 / Hilbert's Program for Mathematics
`数学 / Mathematical`

希尔伯特主张数学能够被无遗漏且无矛盾地形式化封闭；哥德尔证明了数学不可终结，每划定一套系统都会生成新命题。

Hilbert proposed that all mathematical truths could be completely and consistently formalized; Gödel proved formal mathematics is unclosable, forever generating unprovable truths.

→ [Why Mathematics Can Never Be Solved](../../posts/why-mathematics-can-never-be-solved/)

### 内在形而上学 / Immanent Metaphysics
`哲学 / Philosophical`

内在形而上学以三个不可互换的模态为一切领域建模，以类与实例的循环把它们绑在一起，以不可通约定理表明对称与连续不能同时完美，这在拆解二元论与决定论时有用；把三分法则当作现实的地基，在此到了边界，循环是为了修补切分所割断的连续而造的机械。

Immanent metaphysics models every domain through three non-interchangeable modalities, binds them with a cycle of class and instance, and shows through the Incommensuration Theorem that symmetry and continuity cannot both be perfect, of use in taking apart dualism and determinism; taking the law of triplication as the bedrock of reality is where it reaches its boundary, since the cycle is machinery built to mend the continuity the cut severed.

→ [切分的三重影子 / The Three Shadows of the Cut](../../posts/the-three-shadows-of-the-cut/)

### 帝国崩溃演化律 / Imperial Collapse Dynamics
`意识形态 / Ideological`

把过度扩张、财政枯竭、制度衰败当作帝国崩溃的规律性原因，在为历史痕迹命名时有用；它在此到了边界：同样的标签解释不了各不相同的崩溃，帝国是许多中心的对齐被压缩到一个容量有限的位点上，崩溃来自自我形象被豁免了再校准。

Treating overstretch, fiscal exhaustion and institutional decay as lawlike causes of imperial collapse is of use in naming historical traces; it reaches its boundary in that the same labels cannot explain collapses that differ, since an empire is the alignment of many centres compressed onto a locus of finite capacity, and collapse comes from a self-image exempted from recalibration.

→ [战争、革命与帝国的衰落 / War, Revolution, and the Fall of Empires](../../posts/war-revolution-and-the-fall-of-empires/) · [维度即投影 / Dimensions Are Projections](../../posts/dimensions-are-projections/)

### 信息本体优先论 / Information as Ontologically Primary
`哲学 / Philosophical`

以信息为本、以物质为本，或视二者为双面，在严谨地发展一种描述、取得经验上的用处时有用；它在此到了边界：每一种立场都把自己的出发前提豁免于它施加给对手的审视，一种选择性的描述便呈现得仿佛并非选择，既有自洽的外观，也留下残余的不一致。

Taking information or matter as primary, or both as dual aspects, is of use in developing a description with rigor and empirical use; it reaches its boundary in that each position exempts its own starting premise from the scrutiny it applies to rivals, so a selective description presents itself as not selective, producing both the appearance of coherence and residual incoherence.

→ [选择、矛盾与开放的现实 / Selection, Contradiction, and Open Reality](../../posts/selection-contradiction-and-open-reality/) · [信息是什么 / What Information Is](../../posts/what-information-is/)

### 制度决定论 / Institutional Determinism
`经济 / Economic`

制度解释假定好制度造就能人；制度却是先前自我选择的沉淀，作为约束的描述有用，因果次序仍在做选择的个人一侧。

Institutional explanations assume good institutions produce capable people; institutions are sediment of prior self-selection, so they remain useful descriptions of constraints while the causal order stays with choosing individuals.

→ [The Average Is Residue, Not the Engine](../../posts/the-average-is-residue-not-the-engine/)

### 软件领域的杰文斯悖论 / Jevons Paradox in Software
`经济 / Economic`

杰文斯悖论指出效率提升降低单耗会引爆弹性需求；能解释市场规模爆发，却不描摹具体工种的质性重组。

Jevons paradox shows efficiency gains lower unit resource costs and trigger elastic demand; useful for market scaling, though blind to qualitative role redefinitions.

→ [AI Won't Kill Software Engineering—It Will Expand It](../../posts/ai-wont-kill-software-engineering/)

### 凯恩斯财政乘数理论 / Keynesian Fiscal Multiplier
`经济 / Economic`

凯恩斯主义宣扬财政赤字能凭空创造净财富乘数；政府花的每一分钱都预先借由征税或通胀抽取自民间，挪用了实业积累。

Keynesian economics asserts state deficits create net capital out of nothing; every government dollar is first extracted via taxation, debt, or debasement, diverting private capital.

→ [Public Spending Does Not Create Capital](../../posts/public-spending-does-not-create-capital/)

### 自由放任资本主义 / Laissez-faire Capitalism
`经济 / Economic`

自由放任资本主义把自愿生产与交易的秩序命名为一套经济制度，在描述一片不施强制的协作场域时有用；它在此到了边界：这个名称是事后的命名，不是引擎，不是这套制度产生自由的个人，而是认出自身能动、不发起强制的个人，产生了后来被冠以此名的样式。

Laissez-faire capitalism names the order of voluntary production and trade as an economic system, which is of use in describing a field of coordination free of initiated force; it reaches its boundary in that the name comes after the fact and is no engine, since the system does not produce free individuals but individuals who recognize their own agency and initiate no force produce the pattern later given that name.

→ [能动性的错配 / The Misallocation of Agency](../../posts/the-misallocation-of-agency/)

### 拉普拉斯钟表决定论 / Laplacian Clockwork Determinism
`科学 / Scientific`

钟表决定论假定当前状态加上固定的规则就能推出一切后来的状态；它在把规则固定住的那个心智之处到了边界，模型锁定正是把这幅图装进了自身，作为在规则可视为不变的范围里做预测的工具，它依然好用。

Clockwork determinism assumes the present state plus fixed rules yields every later state; it reaches its boundary at the Mind holding the rules fixed, which is what model-lock installs inside oneself, while as a tool for prediction where the rules can be treated as unchanging it stays useful.

→ [决定论机器 / The Deterministic Machine](../../posts/the-deterministic-machine/)

### 拉普拉斯决定论 / Laplacian Determinism
`科学 / Scientific`

假定掌握微观质点的初始状态与运动定律便能推知未来全部历史；在经典力学中引人入胜，却将世界的开放性转嫁给了无人能给出的初始条件。

Presumes complete knowledge of initial positions and velocities determines all future history; mathematically inspiring, it hides openness in ungrounded initial conditions.

→ [没有固定者的固定 / Fixed by Nothing](../../posts/fixed-by-nothing/)

### 差异制造与因果解释决定论 / Difference-Making and Deterministic Explanation
`科学 / Scientific`

差异制造假定在定律与先前状态给定下，客观贡献可从反事实结果中读出；在封闭工程沙盘中有效，但输入与运作的边界必须先由外部视界给定，定律本身无法推导出自身被切分的前提。

Difference-making presumes that under given laws and prior conditions, causal contribution can be read off by counterfactual variation; effective within closed engineering models, its boundaries between input and operation must be stipulated from an external stance that the laws themselves cannot derive.

→ [信任读者是最后的越界 / Trusting the Reader Is the Last Overstep](../../posts/trusting-the-reader-is-the-last-overstep/)

### 解放叙事的俘虏身份陷阱 / Liberationist Identity Trap
`意识形态 / Ideological`

解放运动自许为拯民于水火的正义使者；高筑解救者人设的前提，却是先把所指称的受众死死钉在任人宰割的受害者耻辱柱上。

Liberation movements present themselves as selfless deliverers from oppression; proclaiming oneself a liberator requires first fixing the client population into a helpless captive identity.

→ [Liberation Rhetoric Begins by Defining Captivity](../../posts/liberation-rhetoric-begins-by-defining-captivity/)

### 乘法过程产生的对数正态分布 / Log-normal Distribution from Multiplicative Processes
`数学 / Mathematical`

近似正态的对数增量逐步累加，使水平成为对数正态分布，这在描述复利下水平如何铺展时有用；它在此到了边界：它的右尾在有限尺度上常读作幂律，而正态与厚尾只是同一个递推的暂时视图，不是两条规律。

Summing approximately normal log increments makes the level log-normal, which is of use in describing how levels spread under compounding; it reaches its boundary in that its right tail frequently reads as a power law on finite scales, while the normal and the heavy tail are transient views of one recurrence, not two laws.

→ [幂律是正态分布的长期后果 / Power Law Is the Long-Term Consequence of Normal Distribution](../../posts/power-law-is-the-long-term-consequence-of-normal-distribution/)

### 逻辑斯蒂增长与S曲线 / Logistic Growth and S-Curves
`科学 / Scientific`

经济预测假定技术发展必将顺着固定的S曲线趋于饱和；饱和上限反映的仅是特定坐标系的承载边界，而非人类创新的绝境。

Economic forecasting assumes technological development must saturate along fixed S-curves; logistic ceilings reflect the limits of a fixed coordinate frame, not creative frontiers.

→ [Closed Assumptions Squeeze Compounding into S-Curves](../../posts/closed-assumptions-squeeze-compounding-into-s-curves/) · [可延展的地平线 / The Extendable Horizon](../../posts/the-extendable-horizon/)

### 无损信息捕获的不可能性 / Lossless Information Capture
`数学 / Mathematical`

拉普拉斯认识论假定能无损记录万物状态以预测未来；开放的生成性现实超越任何有限编码容量，无损全知在数学上不成立。

Laplacian epistemology assumes complete state capture allows flawless future prediction; an open-ended generative field outstrips finite encoding, making complete capture impossible.

→ [Lossless Knowledge of an Open Field Is Incoherent](../../posts/lossless-knowledge-of-an-open-field-is-incoherent/)

### 劳动总量固定谬误 / Lump of Labor Fallacy
`经济 / Economic`

该谬误假定社会所需工作总量是恒定的；常规文案工作的自动化，会释放协调资源去开辟全新的协作领域。

The fallacy presumes there is a fixed quantum of useful work in an economy; automating established clerical tasks frees coordination energy for newly imaginable problems.

→ [When Abundance Looks Like Scarcity](../../posts/when-abundance-looks-like-scarcity/)

### 现代投资组合理论 / Modern Portfolio Theory
`经济 / Economic`

现代投资组合理论以钟形曲线描述回报，在分散配置、控制波动时有用；它在此到了边界：它的钟形与批评它的厚尾一样，都是对已经发生的结果的统计描述，不是可以据以设计策略的生成逻辑。

Modern portfolio theory describes returns with a bell curve, which is of use in diversifying and controlling volatility; it reaches its boundary in that its bell curve, like the heavy tail its critics oppose to it, is a statistical description of outcomes already produced, not the generative logic to design strategy around.

→ [幂律是结果的表象 / Power Law Is the Surface of Outcomes](../../posts/mi-lv-shi-jie-guo-de-biao-xiang/)

### 以买方垄断论证工资下限 / Monopsony Justification for Wage Floors
`经济 / Economic`

以法令设定工资的框架需要不断补充关于市场力量与需求无弹性的假设；它能贴合局部案例，却要不断调整以应对方向性的失业证据。

Frameworks setting wages by decree need continual assumptions about market power and inelastic demand; they can fit local cases while requiring retuning against directional disemployment evidence.

→ [The Misallocation of Cause in Wealth-Transfer Policies and Price Floors](../../posts/the-reversal-of-causality-in-wealth-transfer-policies-and-price-floors/)

### 朴素实在论 / Naive Realism
`哲学 / Philosophical`

朴素实在论假定观察者共享一个未经中介的场景，在日常协作中作为默认的出发点有用；它在此到了边界：共享的现实是私人投影之间很高的功能共振，足以支撑行动、导航与语言，却从不是经验的同一。

Naive realism assumes observers share an unmediated scene, which is of use as the default starting point of everyday coordination; it reaches its boundary in that shared reality is high functional resonance among private projections, robust enough for action, navigation and language yet never identity of experience.

→ [表征的闭环 / The Closed Loop of Representation](../../posts/the-closed-loop-of-representation/)

### 消极功利主义（最小化可避免的苦难） / Negative Utilitarianism (Minimizing Avoidable Suffering)
`哲学 / Philosophical`

最小化可避免的苦难，把苦难当作可由外部力量减去的对象，把原因移离登记它的中心；作为政策启发可用，却会再生它所针对的落差。

Minimizing avoidable suffering treats suffering as an object external agency can subtract, relocating cause away from the registering center; useful as policy heuristic, it regenerates the gap it targets.

→ [The Initial Distinction, where the rationalism attempted to and failed to escape irrationality](../../posts/the-initial-distinction/)

### 神经网络缩放定律 / Neural Scaling Laws
`技术 / Technological`

Kaplan 与 Chinchilla 的缩放定律把既有产物当作待压缩的给定语料，在安排预训练中参数与数据的配比时有用；它们在此到了边界：语料本身在回路中不断扩张，权重只是当前集合的一张有限快照，把它当作上限，就冻结了回路的一个旋钮。

The Kaplan and Chinchilla scaling laws treat existing artifacts as a given corpus to compress, which is of use in allocating parameters and data in pre-training; they reach their boundary in that the corpus itself keeps expanding in the loop, and the weights are a finite snapshot of the current set, so taking them as the limit freezes one dial of the loop.

→ [规模化回路 / The Scaling Loop](../../posts/the-scaling-loop/)

### 没有免费午餐定理 / No Free Lunch Theorem
`数学 / Mathematical`

该定理证明在所有可能函数空间算法表现均等；形式上严密，但套用到受物理规律约束的现实世界则失真。

The theorem proves uniform algorithm parity over all mathematically possible functions; strictly true in formal bounds, it misleads when applied to physics-constrained real worlds.

→ [The Shadow Mistaken for the Object: Chollet's Intelligence Explosion Argument](../../posts/chollet-intelligence-explosion-analysis/)

### 正态分布 / Normal Distribution
`科学 / Scientific`

正态分布在描述大量离散决策在短窗口里聚集于均值附近时有用；它在此到了边界：它是自由选择事后沉淀出的统计表象，反映的是各心智提取信号能力的不均，对任何一个个体此刻的选择没有因果约束力，被读作不存在真实差异或内在随机，就遮蔽了乘法过程将放大的东西。

The normal distribution is of use in describing how many discrete decisions gather near the mean over a short window; it reaches its boundary in that it is the statistical appearance free choices leave after the fact, reflecting uneven signal-extraction capacity across Minds, with no causal binding force on any individual's choice now, and read as evidence of no real differences or of intrinsic randomness, it hides what multiplication will magnify.

→ [幂律是正态分布的长期后果 / Power Law Is the Long-Term Consequence of Normal Distribution](../../posts/power-law-is-the-long-term-consequence-of-normal-distribution/) · [目的性的评判与认知的缺省状态 / Judging by Purpose and the Default State of Cognition](../../posts/cong-mu-di-xing-de-ping-pan-kan-ren-zhi-que-sheng/) · [观察的因果僭越与第一人称的收益差 / The Causal Overstep of Observation and the First-Person Surplus](../../posts/the-causal-overstep-of-observation-and-the-first-person-surplus/)

### 观察性行为学 / Observational Behavioral Science
`科学 / Scientific`

将群体在事后沉淀的统计分布逆向设定为微观决策的准入门槛；遗漏了行为选择仅发生在第一人称对净收益与摩擦的具身体感之中，在描述人口学频次与精算规划时保持有效。

Reverses retrospective population distributions into gatekeeping requirements for micro-decisions; it misses that action occurs solely within first-person reckoning of surplus over friction, remaining useful for demographic accounting and actuarial planning.

→ [观察的因果僭越与第一人称的收益差 / The Causal Overstep of Observation and the First-Person Surplus](../../posts/the-causal-overstep-of-observation-and-the-first-person-surplus/)

### OntoAgent认知架构 / OntoAgent Cognitive Architecture
`技术 / Technological`

符号架构预设意义可在形式本体中预先登记；在受控领域内稳健，一旦面对未预设语境便失去弹性。

Symbolic architectures assume meaning can be preregistered in formal ontologies; while robust for controlled domains, they freeze once meeting unscripted context.

→ [Agents in the Long Game of AI — Through the Ontological Clarity Framework](../../posts/agents_leia_ontological_clarity/)

### 实践摩擦改变信念机制 / Operational Belief Transformation
`哲学 / Philosophical`

理性辩论预设摆出推演逻辑就能使人折服；根深蒂固的前提大多只在现实撞击导致既有图式不再奏效时，才被迫重构。

Rationalist debate assumes presenting logical counter-arguments changes conviction; deeply held premises shift only when direct reality friction makes previous beliefs unworkable.

→ [How Belief Actually Changes](../../posts/how-belief-actually-changes/)

### 协调客观还原理论 / Orchestrated Objective Reduction (Orch-OR)
`科学 / Scientific`

协调客观还原理论假定波函数坍缩为意识提供所需的不可计算性，把测量问题与困难问题合并；它仍是对单一系统开放性的物理解释。

Orch-OR assumes wavefunction collapse supplies the non-computability consciousness needs, joining measurement and hard problems; it remains a physical account of one system's openness.

→ [On Closed Systems, Open Minds, and the Limits of Proof](../../posts/on-closed-systems-open-minds-and-the-limits-of-proof/) · [另一种相干 / The Other Coherence](../../posts/the-other-coherence/)

### 泛心论 / Panpsychism
`哲学 / Philosophical`

泛心论把主观意识赋予电子等基本粒子；把一切想成以不同速度思考的心智，可以想象，却仍是人的心智按自己所知的样子造出的概念，说电子有意识或没有，都是一个心智把它纳入自己的感知、放下了割点，它在这里到了边界，作为提醒意识不必只在人类大脑皮层里寻找的思路，它依然有用。

Panpsychism attributes conscious awareness to fundamental particles such as electrons; everything as a mind thinking at a different speed can be imagined, yet it is still a concept the human Mind makes after the pattern it knows, and saying the electron is conscious or is not takes it into a Mind's own perception and places the cut, which is where the view reaches its boundary, while as a reminder not to look for consciousness only in the human cortex it stays useful.

→ [当电子开始思考 / When Electrons Think](../../posts/when-electrons-think/) · [折叠的连续谱 / The Continuum of the Fold](../../posts/the-continuum-of-the-fold/)

### 物理主义的无处之境错觉 / Physicalism as View from Nowhere
`哲学 / Philosophical`

物理主义声称无主体的物质世界先于并创造心智；客观的物理公式恰恰建立在执行测量、记录数据的那位不可还原的观察者之上。

Physicalism claims third-person matter precedes and generates mind; objective physical formulas depend upon the irreducible first-person observer who performs the experiments and readings.

→ [因果的自反性与物理的无源假定 / Causality is Irreducible; The Physical is a View from Nowhere](../../posts/causality-is-irreducible-the-physical-is-a-view-from-nowhere/)

### 平台恶化理论 / Platform Enshittification
`经济 / Economic`

平台恶化论把服务衰落归咎于贪婪；平台实则是完成双边锁定后，依自身演化逻辑回收前期的用户补贴。

The enshittification model attributes platform decline to executive greed; platforms complete their lock-in lifecycle by monetizing previously subsidised user surpluses.

→ [The Platform Doesn't Drift — It Completes](../../posts/platform-decision-capture/)

### 普朗克尺度 / Planck Scale
`科学 / Scientific`

普朗克时间与普朗克长度标出现有模型的分辨力用尽的尺度，c = ℓ_P / t_P 把它们与光速连在一起，在计算中有用；把它们读成刻在宇宙容器上、先于观察的最短时长与最短距离，在此到了边界，它们描述的是观察者自己由因果步长织成的感知分辨率。

The Planck time and Planck length mark the scale where present models run out of resolution, and c = ℓ_P / t_P ties them to the speed of light, of use in calculation; reading them as a shortest duration and distance engraved on a cosmic container prior to observation is where they reach their boundary, since they describe the perceptual resolution of an observer whose own apparatus is woven from causal steps.

→ [时间是因果的不可逆方向与离散步长 / Time Is the Irreversible Direction and Discrete Step of Causality](../../posts/time-is-causality-not-a-dimension/)

### 幂律分布 / Power Law Distribution
`科学 / Scientific`

幂律在描述某个观测窗口里结果的形状时有用；它在此到了边界：它被升格为必须服从的规律时，聚合的痕迹被误认作活动本身，而生成它的是复利的速率差。

The power law is of use in describing the shape of outcomes within an observation window; it reaches its boundary in that, once raised into a law that must be obeyed, aggregated traces are taken for the activity itself, while what generates them is the differential of compounding rates.

→ [幂律是结果的表象 / Power Law Is the Surface of Outcomes](../../posts/mi-lv-shi-jie-guo-de-biao-xiang/) · [幂律是正态分布的长期后果 / Power Law Is the Long-Term Consequence of Normal Distribution](../../posts/power-law-is-the-long-term-consequence-of-normal-distribution/) · [可延展的地平线 / The Extendable Horizon](../../posts/the-extendable-horizon/)

### 人的行动学 / Praxeology
`经济 / Economic`

行动学预设一切经济价值源于个体有目的行动；有效抵御机械加总论，却抽离了生物性与制度层面的强迫。

Praxeology presumes purposeful human action underlies economic value; invaluable against mechanical aggregates, it brackets biological and institutional coercion.
